Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any analytical_chemistry module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 320. The projective resolution has length at most 238 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_320(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 320. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^238(S, F) is trivial. QED.
